# Oblivion quest-run worklog (2026-11-11 ..), organized by quest

Companion to [mwscript_full_runs.md](mwscript_full_runs.md). **Every standing rule there applies here
unchanged** (user, 11-12: "follow all the same morrowind test rules"): fix-and-resume, 3 passes per changed
case, no route-forcing legs, place the player when no follower is along, `approach`-style placement instead of
waiting for an NPC, SetDisposition for persuasion, generic fixes only, no docs/tests before in-game confirmation.

## 🛑 Lessons specific to TES4 runs (read before touching a case)
- **The user has played by hand from chargen up to delivering Martin to Cloud Ruler Temple (end of MQ04), and
  no further** (user, 11-12). Up to there a failure is the CASE or the HARNESS first (user, 11-12, twice): don't
  reach for the converter until the case does what a player does. **From MQ05 on nothing is hand-verified and
  "it likely has more bugs than we have seen so far"**: treat a failure as a likely conversion bug, find its
  cause from the snapshot and the export, fix it generically, and never reshape the case to get past it.
- **Don't redo Morrowind's work.** Its runner solved timing, placement, dialogue and walking; look there first.
  🛑 User, 11-13 ("again"): a step Morrowind's cases have (`follow`, `defend`, ...) must work in every game's
  cases through the SAME code -- it is base Skyrim functionality. Never write a second implementation or a
  case-side stand-in (repeated `player.moveto`, a new hook): expose MorrowindRuntime's own call (an export taking
  runtime FormIDs, as `defend` / `pace` / `follow` do) and bind it in tes/test_game.cpp.
- **Never place the player INSIDE a trigger box.** A moveto/setpos into one throws the player elsewhere in the
  cell (chargen cell trigger: landed on CGMarkerAmbushAGlenroy / Renote's marker, 2,600 away). Walk in, as the
  Morroblivion dock case does.
- **A walk "to" a trigger can stop short.** `walk` arrives within 128 on the ground plane; Oblivion's
  TrigZone02 box is 410 x 41 x 292 (bhkBoxShape 2.94/0.29/2.09 x 70), so walk to a floor point PAST it.
- **A topic's result script runs after the answer's last line**: check what it sets with `until`, not `expect`.
- **Skyrim lists a line's new topics only once the line is spoken**: `ask` now waits for its topic.
- **Ask after a greeting's LAST line**: a click between two lines of a multi-line greeting is lost (2r).
- **No case boundary near a greeting**: the harness's checkpoint save (even a failed one, while chargen holds the
  controls) closes an open or opening conversation (1w, 1x, 2b, 2d). Keep a force-greet, its topics and any menu
  re-greet in ONE case, with the boundary at a quiet moment (before a fight, after the last topic).
- **A force-greeter is walked up to, not teleported to**: a teleport into a Skyrim ForceGreet's range gave no
  greeting or lost the menu (1q, 1r, 1t, 1u); a short `walk <npc>` (his greeting ends it) works (1v).
- **Class/answer lines have variants**: `hear` the words they share -- and that NO EARLIER untaken line says
  ("rats and goblins" also matched the answer before the menu, 2i; use "any trouble").
- **Skyrim keeps the Dialogue Menu open under a TES4_Chargen.Ask menu** (2i).
- **Doors: check XLOC before routing through one.** Level 100 + no key = only a script opens it.
- **A case boundary must be a moment where the quest WAITS FOR THE PLAYER.** The game keeps running between a
  resumed save's load and the case's first step (at test pace a whole fight and force-greet fit there, 2g).
- **Saves fail while chargen has controls disabled** (`checkpoint ... NOT SAVED`): resume from the last case
  that saved, not the failed one.

## Launch
- Build: `test_instance --instance N build --mod Oblivion.esm --mod TESGameSelect` (without TESGameSelect there
  is no game picker: "no game selector prompt after New Game").
- New game: `questtest --cases tes_runtime/tes/testdata/quests/oblivion_main --new-game 1 --fast --checkpoints
  --stop-on-fail --timeout 3600` (button 1 = Cyrodiil). Resume: `--from <case>`; one case: `--from X --to X`.
- `--fast` already runs `tgm`; a case must not toggle it again.
- Live probes: `python -m tools.live.tes4_console --instance N` (`!cmd` raw, `@HEXREF cmd` at a ref, else TES4 names resolved).
- Fixed 11-12: the harness swapped console keywords that are also Oblivion EditorIDs (`z` -> 0517D41C, `player`
  -> 05000007): tes4_cases.CONSOLE_WORDS keeps player/x/y/z. A `run` line the engine rejects with a message
  (not "unknown command") does NOT fail the case -- check placements with an `expect`.
- A cross-cell `moveto` lands a few frames later: a `setpos` in the same `run` block is overridden. Put `wait`
  between them.

| -- | -- | -- | POLL PILE-UP, fix sketch (NOT built; the poll is emitted inline in OnUpdate by script_convert/assemble.py `poll` + converter.py `_GAMEMODE_GATE`, lines ~902 / ~1334): (1) move the poll body into its own function so every TES4 `return` leaves through one place; (2) OnUpdate keeps a real-time stamp of the run in progress -- a run that finds a younger-than-N-seconds stamp only re-arms the 5 s watchdog and returns, touching no other script; (3) the stamp is cleared when the body function returns, and its age bound lets a poll whose run aborted start again. Blast radius = every converted script with a GameMode block, so it needs the script tests, a compile of all three plugins and a new-game run before and after. # GENERIC MECHANISMS (harness / runner)

## ONE runner (merged 11-12, user: "use mwtest syntax and code")
- common/test_runner.cpp is the only step loop: every MW step/rule (approach, defend, follow, rest, punch, steer,
  pace, walk restart/rescue, door reach, held-menu names, button settle, bye settle 30) + the TES4 parts (harness
  run/judge, hear, ask waits for its topic). MorrowindRuntime plugin/test_backend.cpp = in-game MWScript judge +
  its Hooks(); TESRuntime tes/test_game.cpp = its game calls. .tes4test == .mwtest grammar.
- Check: MW ingame 187/187 on the merged runner (temp/mw_ingame_merge_check.out); all MW test exes pass.

## Charactergen: Emperor walk, Baurus (0030, 0040) -- Emperor walk PASSING (1v); Baurus OPEN
- baurus_speech is ONE case from marker J (68, quest waits for the player) to MQ02 20: amulet, fight, 86
  force-greet, topics, class menu, 87 re-greet. walk_marker_j / walk_emperor_amulet / baurus_emperor_dies merged
  into it (2f, 2g: everything after 70 ran in the load gap or the save).
- The 87 re-greet is the fragment's `partner.EvaluatePackage()` after TES4_Chargen.Ask returns (force-greet
  CGBaurusToPlayerB). The pick is FinishClass, the same as the OK click.
- Sewer key checked with `until 900` (real pace).
- 🛑 OPEN (conversion suspect): Baurus's player-targeted force-greets (CGBaurusToPlayerA ==86, B ==87; ForceGreet
  template 0003C1C4, topic GREETING_Charactergen 010000C8, SingleRef player, trigger PLDT r200 / r500) start in
  ~60% of runs. When stuck, only ambient HELO barks play and player activation opens NO dialogue (2t). The
  converted package's inputs match vanilla's (MS05InductionForcegreet). Not explained by pace (2q at pace 1),
  pick speed (2n), or the case boundary.
  LIVE (2u, kept game, stage 87): GetIsCurrentPackage CGBaurusToPlayerB = 1, AI procedure 17, not in combat, not
  in dialogue, not talking, detects the player, 70 from the player and NEVER MOVES (same x/y over minutes, game
  time advancing). Player moved to 300 away: still no approach, no greet. `sae IdleForceDefaultState` on him: no
  change. Activation opens no dialogue. A player would be stuck the same way.
- 🛑 WHAT THE 87 RUNS ESTABLISHED (2v-3p; read before another attempt):
  1. With the dialogue left open (HEAD), Baurus says the stage-87 greeting INSIDE the old "The sewers?" dialogue
     2-5 ticks after the pick, every time (2l-2n, 3j-3p; 3h: at the goodbye line's end when the script did not
     wait). The dialogue closes when that line ends, its Choice (CGBaurusF) is never listed, the say-once greeting
     is spent, and `talk` then opens nothing. Deterministic.
  2. Closing the dialogue first (2o change) leaves him silent on CGBaurusToPlayerB (sandbox chatter) in 7 of 8;
     a later evp (3d, 3f, 3i), a runtime evp, moving the player out of r500 and back (3g) do not start it.
     CK wiki: the ForceGreet procedure "completes after the actor says the line and exits dialogue".
  3. A parked INFO End fragment freezes the dialogue: it neither closes nor greets (3a, 90 ticks).
  4. The Dialogue Menu does not close under the class menu (3p), and a close message takes effect only at the
     end of the line being spoken (3l, 3p).
  5. NOT the cause: pick speed (2n, 2z), goodbye flag (3n), the script's early evp (3f), range (3g), pace (2q).
  Open question = why a force-greet line spoken in an already-open dialogue ends the dialogue instead of listing
  its choices. Next source: the exe (DialogueMenu / MenuTopicManager after a ForceGreet line), not another run.
  Exe notes (1.6.1170, started): DialogueMenu CloseMenu callback 0x914980 sets the byte at 0x31ad0ee ("player
  asked to leave") and posts a UI message; DialogueMenu::ProcessMessage is 0x9142b0 (reads that byte for message
  type 6); the reader at 0x3ba126 is the crosshair prompt, not dialogue. BGSProcedureForceGreet vtable 0x1852880:
  its own slots are thin (slot 12 = 0x46b900 checks the actor handle, calls its slot 9 then 0x45bca0); the greet
  itself runs behind 0x45bca0: it reads an actor state through 0x69c6d0 (proceeds only for 2..3; <=1 takes the
  0x45bde3 branch, anything else returns), resolves the target handle, skips a deleted/disabled target, asks
  [this+8]->vfunc 0x28, and for a non-player target builds and posts a say request (0x528720, 0x52bc80). Slot 9
  (0x46b7b0) seeds the state on first run (byte +0x40 = 1).
  Exe, read further (11-12): 0x69c6d0 = the actor's process level (AIProcess +0x137); 0/1 (high) does the work in
  0x45c350, 2/3 just finishes. For a player target 0x45c350 walks him into range, then creates a sub-state
  ([this+0x38]) ticked by 0x45ac00. 0x45ac00 WAITS while any of these holds: actor +0xE8 bit 12; process +0x90
  timer > 0; player gate 0x7397b0 false (it is false while the topic manager 0x3137778 +0xB1 is set, i.e. a
  dialogue is properly open -- measured 3r); no parent cell. When they pass it marks itself DONE (+0x28 = 1,
  finishes the procedure) and only THEN calls Actor vfunc 0x41 (0x660410, SetDialogueWithPlayer(1, forceGreet,
  topic)). That function returns false WITHOUT greeting when player +0xBE2 bit 1 is set, 0x698f90(player), or the
  topic manager still has a speaker (+0x68 handle resolves; 0x5e1a40) -- and the procedure is already finished, so
  the greeting is lost for good. So a force-greet that comes due in the frames where a dialogue has logically
  ended but is not cleaned up is dropped or lands in the closing menu. temp/greet_gates.py reads all of these
  live. Our CloseMenuNamed posts only a UI hide message; the menu's own exit (0x914980) also sets 0x31ad0ee and
  player +0xBE4 bit 1.
- 🛑 CAUSE (3s-3z, measured): the engine re-picks Baurus's package when the "The sewers?" dialogue ends, and
  ToPlayerB (GetStage == 87, player in range) fires at once, while the old Dialogue Menu is still closing. The
  conversation cannot open into the dying menu, so the line is only spoken (0x660410 falls back to a plain say
  when 0x5e0840 fails; same speaker = "continue" branch), Say Once is spent, the Once-Per-Day package is done and
  nothing ever retries. Not our evp (3v), not fixable by a later evp (3w-3y). Holds for ANY goodbye line whose
  result makes a force-greet by the same NPC valid (3h showed it with no menu at all).
  FIX (built, 3/3 in 4a-4c; not yet confirmed by the user in game, so no test or commentary doc yet): converted
  ForceGreet-template packages get a first condition GetGlobalValue <TES4DialogueOpen> == 0
  (packages/converter.py _dialogue_gate; GLOB from owned_records.create_dialogue_open_global, a master's adopted;
  row `dialogueopen=` in attributes_formid.txt). MorrowindRuntime (game_calls_crime.cpp DialogueGateTick) holds
  that global at 1 while Skyrim's Dialogue Menu is up and, on the frame it has closed, clears it and re-evaluates
  the last speaker (ReevaluatePackage). Scripts and chargen_menu.cpp are at HEAD. NOT gated yet: the
  StartConversation pool packages (dialogue/force_greets.py pool_package). Other plugins pick the gate up at
  their next import.
- 86 (CGBaurusToPlayerA): fails when a line of his own is playing as the greeting is due ("No... Talos save us"
  runs 4.8 s, 86 comes 5 s after 85; or a HELO bark, 3m). Stand-back in the case: 8 of 9. Not solid.
- MQ02 10 vs 20 (2o): 88's fragment sets MQ02 10 then 20 on a stopped quest from inside a dialogue; 20 done, 10
  not. Quest.Start in menu mode defers until the menu closes (CK wiki). Unmeasured; now `until 600` each.
